Implement the following plan:

# Fix: OpenCode mid-turn commits don't create checkpoints

## Context

When OpenCode's agent runs `git commit` mid-turn (e.g., "add a script and commit"), the commit gets an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer but no checkpoint data is written to `entire/checkpoints/v1`. The root cause: OpenCode's transcript file (`.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`) only gets created at turn-end via `opencode export`, but condensation tries to read it at commit time.

Claude Code doesn't have this problem because it writes its transcript to disk continuously in real-time.

## Approach

Make OpenCode implement the existing `TranscriptPreparer` interface, then call `PrepareTranscript` before reading the live transcript in the condensation and hook paths. This ensures the transcript file is created on-demand by running `opencode export` when needed.

## Changes

### 1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go` — Add `PrepareTranscript`

Add method to `OpenCodeAgent`:
```go
func (a *OpenCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
    // No-op if file exists; otherwise extract sessionID from path and call fetchAndCacheExport
}
```
- If file at `sessionRef` already exists → return nil
- Extract session ID from path basename (strip `.json`)
- Delegate to existing `fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID)`
- Add `"strings"` to imports

### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/transcript.go` — Add compile-time assertion

Add `_ agent.TranscriptPreparer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)` to the existing `var` block (line 14-17).

### 3. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` — Two call sites

**Site A: `CondenseSession` (line ~134-143, no-shadow-branch path)**
Before `extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)`, call `PrepareTranscript` via agent registry:
```go
if ag, agErr := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType); agErr == nil {
    if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
        _ = preparer.PrepareTranscript(state.TranscriptPath)
    }
}
```

**Site B: `extractSessionData` (line ~411-414, live transcript preferred path)**
Before `os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath)`, same pattern.

### 4. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — Two call sites

**Site C: `extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript` (line ~1142-1150)**
After `agent.GetByAgentType`, before `analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition`:
```go
if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
    _ = preparer.PrepareTranscript(state.TranscriptPath)
}
```

**Site D: `extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript` (line ~1179-1182)**
Same pattern — after agent lookup, before transcript read.

### 5. Tests

- **Unit test** in `agent/opencode/lifecycle_test.go`: Test `PrepareTranscript` no-op when file exists, error on bad path format
- **Integration test** in `integration_test/`: OpenCode mid-turn commit creates checkpoint on `entire/checkpoints/v1`

## Why this approach

- Uses the existing `TranscriptPreparer` interface — no new abstractions
- Pattern already established by Claude Code (`claudecode/lifecycle.go:124`)
- All call sites are no-ops for Claude Code (file already exists) and Gemini (doesn't implement interface)
- `fetchAndCacheExport` already handles test mocking via `ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT`

## Verification

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`
2. Integration tests cover the full flow: turn-start → mid-turn commit → checkpoint on `entire/checkpoints/v1`

```
     1→package opencode

    15→// Hook name constants — these become CLI subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`.
    16→const (
    17→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
    18→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
    19→	HookNameTurnStart    = "turn-start"
    20→	HookNameTurnEnd      = "turn-end"
    21→	HookNameCompaction   = "compaction"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    25→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    26→	return []string{
    27→		HookNameSessionStart,
    28→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    29→		HookNameTurnStart,
    30→		HookNameTurnEnd,
    31→		HookNameCompaction,
    32→	}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    36→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    37→	switch hookName {
    38→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    39→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    40→		if err != nil {
    41→			return nil, err
    42→		}
    43→		return &agent.Event{
    44→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    45→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    46→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    47→		}, nil
    48→
    49→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    50→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    51→		if err != nil {
    52→			return nil, err
    53→		}
    54→		// Get the temp file path for this session (may not exist yet, but needed for pre-prompt state).
    55→		repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    56→		if err != nil {
    57→			repoRoot = "."
    58→		}
    59→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    60→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    61→		return &agent.Event{
    62→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    63→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    64→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    65→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    66→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    67→		}, nil
    68→
    69→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    70→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    71→		if err != nil {
    72→			return nil, err
    73→		}
    74→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    75→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    76→		if exportErr != nil {
    77→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    78→		}
    79→		return &agent.Event{
    80→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    81→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    82→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    83→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    84→		}, nil
    85→
    86→	case HookNameCompaction:
    87→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			return nil, err
    90→		}
    91→		return &agent.Event{
    92→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    93→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    94→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    95→		}, nil
    96→
    97→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    98→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    99→		if err != nil {
   100→			return nil, err
   101→		}
   102→		return &agent.Event{
   103→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   104→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   105→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   106→		}, nil
   107→
   108→	default:
   109→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   110→	}
   111→}
   112→
   113→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   114→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   115→//
   116→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   117→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   118→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   119→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   120→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   121→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   122→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		repoRoot = "."
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   128→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   129→
   130→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   131→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   132→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   133→			return tmpFile, nil
   134→		}
   135→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   139→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Validate output is valid JSON before caching
   145→	if !json.Valid(data) {
   146→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export returned invalid JSON (%d bytes)", len(data))
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   150→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   151→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   155→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	return tmpFile, nil
   159→}
   160→
```

```
     1→package opencode

     4→	"encoding/json"

    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Compile-time interface assertions
    14→var (
    15→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    16→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)

    18→
    19→// ParseExportSession parses export JSON content into an ExportSession structure.
    20→func ParseExportSession(data []byte) (*ExportSession, error) {
    21→	if len(data) == 0 {
    22→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil for empty data is expected
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	var session ExportSession
    26→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &session); err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse export session: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	return &session, nil
    31→}
    32→
    33→// parseExportSessionFromFile reads and parses an export JSON transcript file.
    34→func parseExportSessionFromFile(path string) (*ExportSession, error) {
    35→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // Callers check os.IsNotExist on this error
    38→	}
    39→	return ParseExportSession(data)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// SliceFromMessage returns an OpenCode export transcript scoped to messages starting from
    43→// startMessageIndex. This is the OpenCode equivalent of transcript.SliceFromLine —
    44→// for OpenCode's JSON format, scoping is done by message index rather than line offset.
    45→// Returns the original data if startMessageIndex <= 0.
    46→// Returns nil, nil if startMessageIndex exceeds the number of messages.
    47→func SliceFromMessage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) ([]byte, error) {
    48→	if len(data) == 0 || startMessageIndex <= 0 {
    49→		return data, nil
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse export session for slicing: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→	if session == nil {
    57→		return nil, nil
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	if startMessageIndex >= len(session.Messages) {
    61→		return nil, nil
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	scoped := &ExportSession{
    65→		Info:     session.Info,
    66→		Messages: session.Messages[startMessageIndex:],
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	out, err := json.Marshal(scoped)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal scoped session: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→	return out, nil
    74→}
    75→
    76→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of messages in the transcript.
    77→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    78→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    81→			return 0, nil
    82→		}
    83→		return 0, err
    84→	}
    85→	if session == nil {
    86→		return 0, nil
    87→	}
    88→	return len(session.Messages), nil
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified by tool calls from the given message offset.
    92→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
    93→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    96→			return nil, 0, nil
    97→		}
    98→		return nil, 0, err
    99→	}
   100→	if session == nil {
   101→		return nil, 0, nil
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   105→	var files []string
   106→
   107→	for i := startOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   108→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   109→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
   110→			continue
   111→		}
   112→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   113→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   114→				continue
   115→			}
   116→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   117→				continue
   118→			}
   119→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   120→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   121→				seen[filePath] = true
   122→				files = append(files, filePath)
   123→			}
   124→		}
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	return files, len(session.Messages), nil
   128→}
   129→
   130→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts modified file paths from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
   131→// This is the bytes-based equivalent of ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, used by ReadSession.
   132→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   133→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return nil, err
   136→	}
   137→	if session == nil {
   138→		return nil, nil
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   142→	var files []string
   143→
   144→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   145→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
   146→			continue
   147→		}
   148→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   149→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   150→				continue
   151→			}
   152→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   153→				continue
   154→			}
   155→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   156→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   157→				seen[filePath] = true
   158→				files = append(files, filePath)
   159→			}
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	return files, nil
   164→}
   165→
   166→// extractFilePathFromInput extracts the file path from an OpenCode tool's input map.
   167→// OpenCode uses camelCase keys (e.g., "filePath"), with "path" as a fallback.
   168→func extractFilePathFromInput(input map[string]any) string {
   169→	for _, key := range []string{"filePath", "path"} {
   170→		if v, ok := input[key]; ok {
   171→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   172→				return s
   173→			}
   174→		}
   175→	}
   176→	return ""
   177→}
   178→
   179→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompt strings from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   180→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
   181→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   184→			return nil, nil
   185→		}
   186→		return nil, err
   187→	}
   188→	if session == nil {
   189→		return nil, nil
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	var prompts []string
   193→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   194→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   195→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   196→			continue
   197→		}
   198→		// Extract text from parts
   199→		content := ExtractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   200→		if content != "" {
   201→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   202→		}
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	return prompts, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message content as a summary.
   209→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
   210→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   213→			return "", nil
   214→		}
   215→		return "", err
   216→	}
   217→	if session == nil {
   218→		return "", nil
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	for i := len(session.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   222→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   223→		if msg.Info.Role == roleAssistant {
   224→			content := ExtractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   225→			if content != "" {
   226→				return content, nil
   227→			}
   228→		}
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	return "", nil
   232→}
   233→
   234→// ExtractTextFromParts extracts text content from message parts.
   235→func ExtractTextFromParts(parts []Part) string {
   236→	var texts []string
   237→	for _, part := range parts {
   238→		if part.Type == "text" && part.Text != "" {
   239→			texts = append(texts, part.Text)
   240→		}
   241→	}
   242→	return strings.Join(texts, "\n")
   243→}
   244→
   245→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
   246→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path.
   247→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   248→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return nil, err
   251→	}
   252→	if session == nil {
   253→		return nil, nil
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	var prompts []string
   257→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   258→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   259→			continue
   260→		}
   261→		content := ExtractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   262→		if content != "" {
   263→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   264→		}
   265→	}
   266→	return prompts, nil
   267→}
   268→
   269→// CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes computes token usage from raw export JSON transcript bytes
   270→// starting at the given message offset.
   271→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path (which has bytes, not a file path).
   272→func CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   273→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   274→	if err != nil || session == nil {
   275→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   276→	}
   277→
   278→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   279→	for i := startMessageIndex; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   280→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   281→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   282→			continue
   283→		}
   284→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   285→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   286→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   287→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   288→		usage.APICallCount++
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	return usage
   292→}
   293→
   294→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from assistant messages starting at the given offset.
   295→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   296→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   299→			return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for nonexistent file is expected
   300→		}
   301→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript for token usage: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	if session == nil {
   304→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for empty file is expected
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   308→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   309→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   310→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   311→			continue
   312→		}
   313→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   314→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   315→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   316→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   317→		usage.APICallCount++
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return usage, nil
   321→}
```

```
    20→var (

    22→	_ agent.TranscriptPreparer     = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)

    26→
    27→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    28→// These become subcommands: entire hooks claude-code <verb>
    29→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    30→	return []string{
    31→		HookNameSessionStart,
    32→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    33→		HookNameStop,
    34→		HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
    35→		HookNamePreTask,
    36→		HookNamePostTask,
    37→		HookNamePostTodo,
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→// ParseHookEvent translates a Claude Code hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    42→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    43→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    44→	switch hookName {
    45→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    46→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    47→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    48→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    49→	case HookNameStop:
    50→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    51→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    52→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    53→	case HookNamePreTask:
    54→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    55→	case HookNamePostTask:
    56→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    57→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    58→		// PostTodo is Claude-specific; handled outside the generic dispatcher.
    59→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    60→	default:
    61→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    62→	}
    63→}
    64→
    65→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    66→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    67→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→	return data, nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    75→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    76→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	var prompts []string
    82→	for i := range lines {
    83→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    84→			continue
    85→		}
    86→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    87→		if content != "" {
    88→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    89→		}
    90→	}
    91→	return prompts, nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    95→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    96→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   102→	if parseErr != nil {
   103→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
   107→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   108→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   109→			continue
   110→		}
   111→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   112→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   113→			continue
   114→		}
   115→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   116→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   117→				return block.Text, nil
   118→			}
   119→		}
   120→	}
   121→	return "", nil
   122→}
   123→
   124→// PrepareTranscript waits for Claude Code's async transcript flush to complete.
   125→// Claude writes a hook_progress sentinel entry after flushing all pending writes.
   126→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
   127→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   128→	return nil
   129→}

   131→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
   132→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   133→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/
   134→	// but we don't have the sessionID here. The caller should pass the transcript path
   135→	// which may contain the session ID in its directory structure.
   136→	// For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure.
   137→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   138→}

   142→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   143→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   144→	if err != nil {
   145→		return nil, err
   146→	}
   147→	return &agent.Event{

   149→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,

   151→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   152→	}, nil
   153→}

   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   211→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, err
   214→	}
   215→	event := &agent.Event{
   216→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   217→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   218→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   219→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   220→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   221→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   222→	}
   223→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   224→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   225→	}
   226→	return event, nil
   227→}
   228→
   229→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   230→
   231→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   232→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   233→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   234→
   235→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   236→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   237→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   238→	const (
   239→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   240→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   241→		tailBytes    = 4096
   242→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   243→	)
   244→
   245→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   246→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   247→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   248→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   249→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   250→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   251→			)
   252→			return
   253→		}
   254→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   255→	}
   256→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   257→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   258→	)
   259→}
   260→
   261→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   262→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   263→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		return false
   266→	}
   267→	defer f.Close()
   268→
   269→	info, err := f.Stat()
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return false
   272→	}
   273→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   274→	if offset < 0 {
   275→		offset = 0
   276→	}
   277→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   278→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   279→		return false
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   283→	for _, line := range lines {
   284→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   285→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   286→			continue
   287→		}
   288→
   289→		var entry struct {
   290→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   291→		}
   292→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   293→			continue
   294→		}
   295→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   296→		if err != nil {
   297→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   298→			if err != nil {
   299→				continue
   300→			}
   301→		}
   302→		// Validate timestamp is within acceptable range:
   303→		// - Not too far in the past (before hook started minus skew)
   304→		// - Not too far in the future (after hook started plus skew)
   305→		lowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   306→		upperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)
   307→		if ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {
   308→			return true
   309→		}
   310→	}
   311→	return false
   312→}
   313→
```

```
    30→
    31→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    32→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    34→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    45→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    46→	for _, c := range committed {
    47→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    48→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    49→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    50→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    51→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    52→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    53→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    54→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    55→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    56→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    57→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    58→		})
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return result, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    65→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    66→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil, err
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    72→	for _, cp := range all {
    73→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    74→			result = append(result, cp)
    75→		}
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	if len(result) == 0 {
    79→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	return result, nil
    83→}
    84→
    85→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    86→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    87→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    88→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→	if content == nil {
    98→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    99→	}
   100→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   101→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	return content.Transcript, nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   108→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   109→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   110→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   111→//
   112→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveStep called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   113→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   114→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   115→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   116→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   117→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   118→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   119→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   120→
   121→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   122→	if hasShadowBranch {
   123→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   124→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   125→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   126→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   127→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveStep may have been skipped if the
   128→		// last turn had no code changes).
   129→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   130→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   131→		if err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→	} else {
   135→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveStep.
   136→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   137→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   138→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   139→		}
   140→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   143→		}
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   147→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   148→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   149→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   150→		// Track if we had files before filtering to distinguish between:
   151→		// - Session had files but none were committed (don't fallback)
   152→		// - Session had no files to begin with (mid-session commit, fallback OK)
   153→		hadFilesBeforeFiltering := len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0
   154→
   155→		if hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   156→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   157→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   158→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   159→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   160→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   161→				}
   162→			}
   163→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   164→		}
   165→
   166→		// Only use committedFiles as fallback for genuine mid-session commits where
   167→		// no files were tracked yet (extraction returned empty). Do NOT fallback when
   168→		// the session had files that simply didn't overlap with the commit - that
   169→		// indicates an unrelated session that shouldn't have its files_touched
   170→		// overwritten with unrelated committed files.
   171→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 && !hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   172→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   173→			for f := range committedFiles {
   174→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   175→			}
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Get checkpoint store
   180→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   181→	if err != nil {
   182→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get author info
   186→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   187→	// Calculate attribution. When no shadow branch exists (agent committed mid-turn
   188→	// before SaveStep), pass nil ref — the function uses HEAD as the shadow tree
   189→	// since the agent's commit IS HEAD (no user edits between agent work and commit).
   190→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   191→	// Get current branch name
   192→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   193→
   194→	// Generate summary if enabled
   195→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   196→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   197→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   198→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   199→
   200→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   201→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   202→		// For Gemini/OpenCode (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   203→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   204→		switch state.AgentType {
   205→		case agent.AgentTypeGemini:
   206→			scoped, sliceErr := geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   207→			if sliceErr != nil {
   208→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "failed to scope Gemini transcript for summary",
   209→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   210→					slog.String("error", sliceErr.Error()))
   211→			}
   212→			scopedTranscript = scoped
   213→		case agent.AgentTypeOpenCode:
   214→			scoped, sliceErr := opencode.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   215→			if sliceErr != nil {
   216→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "failed to scope OpenCode transcript for summary",
   217→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   218→					slog.String("error", sliceErr.Error()))
   219→			}
   220→			scopedTranscript = scoped
   221→		case agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, agent.AgentTypeUnknown:
   222→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   223→		}
   224→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   225→			var err error
   226→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   227→			if err != nil {
   228→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   229→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   230→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   231→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   232→			} else {
   233→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   234→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   235→			}
   236→		}
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   240→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   241→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   242→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   243→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   244→		Branch:                      branchName,
   245→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   246→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   247→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   248→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   249→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   250→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   251→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   252→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   253→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   254→		TurnID:                      state.TurnID,
   255→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   256→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   257→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   258→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   259→		Summary:                     summary,
   260→	}); err != nil {
   261→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	return &CondenseResult{
   265→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   266→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   267→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   268→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   269→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   270→	}, nil
   271→}
   272→
   273→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   274→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   275→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   276→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   277→	//
   278→	// When shadowRef is nil (agent committed mid-turn before SaveStep),
   279→	// HEAD is used as the shadow tree. This is correct because the agent's
   280→	// commit IS HEAD — there are no user edits between agent work and commit.
   281→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   282→
   283→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   284→	if headErr != nil {
   285→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   286→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   287→		return nil
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   291→	if commitErr != nil {
   292→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   293→			slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   294→		return nil
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   298→	if treeErr != nil {
   299→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   300→			slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   301→		return nil
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	// Get shadow tree: from shadow branch if available, otherwise HEAD (agent committed directly).
   305→	var shadowTree *object.Tree
   306→	if shadowRef != nil {
   307→		shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   308→		if shadowErr != nil {
   309→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   310→				slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   311→				slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   312→			return nil
   313→		}
   314→		var shadowTreeErr error
   315→		shadowTree, shadowTreeErr = shadowCommit.Tree()
   316→		if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   317→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   318→				slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   319→			return nil
   320→		}
   321→	} else {
   322→		// No shadow branch: agent committed mid-turn. Use HEAD as shadow
   323→		// because the agent's work is the commit itself.
   324→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: using HEAD as shadow (no shadow branch)")
   325→		shadowTree = headTree
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Get base tree (state before session started)
   329→	var baseTree *object.Tree
   330→	attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   331→	if attrBase == "" {
   332→		attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   333→	}
   334→	if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   335→		if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   336→			baseTree = tree
   337→		} else {
   338→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   339→				slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   340→		}
   341→	} else {
   342→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   343→			slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   344→			slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   348→	var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   349→	for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   350→		totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   351→		totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   352→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   353→			slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   354→			slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   355→			slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   356→			slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   357→			slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   358→			slog.Int("index", i))
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	attribution := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   362→		baseTree,
   363→		shadowTree,
   364→		headTree,
   365→		sessionData.FilesTouched,
   366→		state.PromptAttributions,
   367→	)
   368→
   369→	if attribution != nil {
   370→		logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   371→			slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   372→			slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   373→			slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   374→			slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   375→			slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   376→			slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   377→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   378→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   379→			slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	return attribution
   383→}
   384→
   385→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   386→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   387→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   388→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   389→// This handles the case where SaveStep was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   390→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   391→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   392→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   393→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   394→	if err != nil {
   395→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   404→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   405→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   406→
   407→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   408→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   409→	// (SaveStep is only called when there are file modifications).
   410→	var fullTranscript string
   411→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   412→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   413→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   414→		}
   415→	}
   416→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   417→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   418→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   419→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   420→				fullTranscript = content
   421→			}
   422→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   423→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   424→				fullTranscript = content
   425→			}
   426→		}
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   430→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   431→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   432→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   433→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   434→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   435→	}
   436→
   437→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   438→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   439→
   440→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   441→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   442→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	return data, nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   449→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   450→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   451→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   452→
   453→	// Read the live transcript
   454→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   455→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   464→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   468→	data.Transcript = liveData
   469→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   470→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   471→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   472→
   473→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   474→	// (no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   475→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   476→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   477→	} else {
   478→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   479→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   483→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   484→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	return data, nil
   488→}
   489→
   490→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   491→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   492→// For Gemini CLI, OpenCode, and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   493→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   494→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   495→	if content == "" {
   496→		return 0
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// OpenCode uses export JSON format with {"info": {...}, "messages": [...]}
   500→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   501→		session, err := opencode.ParseExportSession([]byte(content))
   502→		if err == nil && session != nil {
   503→			return len(session.Messages)
   504→		}
   505→		return 0
   506→	}
   507→
   508→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   509→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   510→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   511→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   512→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   513→		}
   514→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   515→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   516→			return 0
   517→		}
   518→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   522→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   523→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   524→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   525→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   526→	}
   527→	return len(allLines)
   528→}
   529→
   530→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   531→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   532→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   533→	if content == "" {
   534→		return nil
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with a different per-line schema than Claude Code
   538→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   539→		prompts, err := opencode.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   540→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   541→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   542→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   543→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   544→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   545→				}
   546→			}
   547→			return cleaned
   548→		}
   549→		return nil
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   553→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   554→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   555→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   556→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   557→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   558→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   559→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   560→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   561→				}
   562→			}
   563→			return cleaned
   564→		}
   565→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   566→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   567→			return nil
   568→		}
   569→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   573→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   574→}
   575→
   576→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   577→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   578→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   579→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   580→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   581→	if len(data) == 0 {
   582→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with token info on assistant messages (different schema from Claude Code)
   586→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   587→		return opencode.CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data, startOffset)
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   591→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   592→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   593→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   594→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   595→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   596→		}
   597→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   598→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   599→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   600→		}
   601→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   605→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   606→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   607→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   608→	}
   609→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   610→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   611→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   612→	}
   613→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   614→}
   615→
   616→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   617→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   618→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   619→	var prompts []string
   620→	for _, line := range lines {
   621→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   622→		if line == "" {
   623→			continue
   624→		}
   625→
   626→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   627→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   628→			continue
   629→		}
   630→
   631→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   632→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   633→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   634→			continue
   635→		}
   636→
   637→		// Extract message content
   638→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   639→		if !ok {
   640→			continue
   641→		}
   642→
   643→		// Handle string content
   644→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   645→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   646→			if cleaned != "" {
   647→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   648→			}
   649→			continue
   650→		}
   651→
   652→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   653→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   654→			var texts []string
   655→			for _, item := range arr {
   656→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   657→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   658→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   659→							texts = append(texts, text)
   660→						}
   661→					}
   662→				}
   663→			}
   664→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   665→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   666→				if cleaned != "" {
   667→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   668→				}
   669→			}
   670→		}
   671→	}
   672→	return prompts
   673→}
   674→
   675→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   676→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   677→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   678→		return nil
   679→	}
   680→
   681→	var buf strings.Builder
   682→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   683→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   684→
   685→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   686→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   687→		displayPrompt := prompt
   688→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   689→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   690→		}
   691→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   692→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   693→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   694→	}
   695→
   696→	return []byte(buf.String())
   697→}
   698→
   699→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   700→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   701→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   702→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   703→
   704→	// Load session state
   705→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   706→	if err != nil {
   707→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   708→	}
   709→	if state == nil {
   710→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	// Open repository
   714→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   715→	if err != nil {
   716→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   720→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   721→	if err != nil {
   722→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   726→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   727→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   728→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   729→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   730→
   731→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   732→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   733→		// Just clean up the state file.
   734→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   735→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   736→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   737→		)
   738→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   739→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   740→		}
   741→		return nil
   742→	}
   743→
   744→	// Condense the session
   745→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, nil)
   746→	if err != nil {
   747→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   751→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   752→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   753→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   754→	)
   755→
   756→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   757→	state.StepCount = 0
   758→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   759→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   760→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   761→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   762→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   763→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   764→
   765→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   766→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   767→	}
   768→
   769→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   770→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   771→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   772→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   773→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   774→		)
   775→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	return nil
   779→}
   780→
   781→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   782→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   783→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   784→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   785→	if err != nil {
   786→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   787→	}
   788→
   789→	for _, state := range allStates {
   790→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   791→			continue
   792→		}
   793→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   794→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   795→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   796→			return nil
   797→		}
   798→	}
   799→
   800→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   801→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   802→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   803→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   804→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   805→			return nil
   806→		}
   807→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   808→	}
   809→	return nil
   810→}
   811→
```

```
    31→// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
    32→// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
    33→//
    34→// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
    35→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
    36→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
    37→func hasTTY() bool {
    38→	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
    39→		return v == "1"
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands.
    43→	// Gemini subprocesses may have access to the user's TTY, but they can't
    44→	// actually respond to interactive prompts. Treat them as non-TTY.
    45→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    46→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    47→		return false
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return false
    53→	}
    54→	_ = tty.Close()
    55→	return true
    56→}
    57→
    58→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    59→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    60→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    61→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    62→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    63→	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
    64→	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
    65→	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
    66→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
    67→		return defaultYes
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands (including git commit).
    71→	// The agent can't respond to TTY prompts, so use the default to avoid hanging.
    72→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    73→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    74→		return defaultYes
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    78→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    79→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    82→		return defaultYes
    83→	}
    84→	defer tty.Close()
    85→
    86→	// Show context if provided
    87→	if context != "" {
    88→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    92→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    93→	var hint string
    94→	if defaultYes {
    95→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    96→	} else {
    97→		hint = "[y/N]"
    98→	}
    99→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
   100→
   101→	// Read response
   102→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
   103→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		return defaultYes
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
   109→	switch response {
   110→	case "y", "yes":
   111→		return true
   112→	case "n", "no":
   113→		return false
   114→	default:
   115→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
   116→		return defaultYes
   117→	}
   118→}
   119→
   120→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   121→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
   122→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
   123→//
   124→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   125→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
   126→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	message := string(content)
   132→
   133→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   134→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
   135→		// No trailer, nothing to do
   136→		return nil
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
   140→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
   141→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
   142→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
   143→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   144→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	return nil
   149→}
   150→
   151→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   152→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   153→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   154→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   155→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   156→		// Skip empty lines
   157→		if trimmed == "" {
   158→			continue
   159→		}
   160→		// Skip comment lines
   161→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   162→			continue
   163→		}
   164→		// Skip our trailer line
   165→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   166→			continue
   167→		}
   168→		// Found user content
   169→		return true
   170→	}
   171→	return false
   172→}
   173→
   174→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   175→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   176→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   177→	var result []string
   178→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   179→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   180→			result = append(result, line)
   181→		}
   182→	}
   183→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   184→}
   185→
   186→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   187→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   188→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   189→//
   190→// Detects:
   191→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   192→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   193→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   194→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   195→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   196→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Check for rebase state directories
   202→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   203→		return true
   204→	}
   205→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   206→		return true
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   210→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   211→		return true
   212→	}
   213→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   214→		return true
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	return false
   218→}
   219→
   220→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   221→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   222→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   223→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   224→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   225→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   226→//
   227→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   228→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   229→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   230→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   231→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from LastCheckpointID
   232→//
   233→
   234→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   235→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   236→
   237→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   238→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   239→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   240→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   241→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   242→			slog.String("source", source),
   243→		)
   244→		return nil
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   248→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   249→	switch source {
   250→	case "merge", "squash":
   251→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   252→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   253→			slog.String("source", source),
   254→		)
   255→		return nil
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   259→	if source == "commit" {
   260→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   269→	if err != nil {
   270→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   274→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   275→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   276→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   277→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   278→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   279→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   280→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   281→			slog.String("source", source),
   282→		)
   283→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
   287→	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
   288→	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
   289→	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
   290→	// add the trailer directly.
   291→	if !hasTTY() {
   292→		for _, state := range sessions {
   293→			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   294→				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
   295→			}
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   300→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   301→
   302→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   303→		// No new content — no trailer needed
   304→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   305→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   306→			slog.String("source", source),
   307→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   308→		)
   309→		return nil
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Read current commit message
   313→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	message := string(content)
   319→
   320→	// Check if trailer already exists (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   321→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   322→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   323→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   324→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   325→			slog.String("source", source),
   326→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   327→		)
   328→		return nil
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Generate a fresh checkpoint ID
   332→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   333→	if err != nil {
   334→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   338→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   339→	var lastPrompt string
   340→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   341→		firstSession := sessionsWithContent[0]
   342→		if firstSession.AgentType != "" {
   343→			agentType = firstSession.AgentType
   344→		}
   345→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, firstSession)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   349→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   350→
   351→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   352→	switch source {
   353→	case "message":
   354→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   355→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   356→
   357→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   358→		var promptContext string
   359→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   360→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   361→		}
   362→
   363→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   364→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   365→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   366→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   367→				slog.String("source", source),
   368→			)
   369→			return nil
   370→		}
   371→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   372→	default:
   373→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   374→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   378→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   379→		slog.String("source", source),
   380→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   381→	)
   382→
   383→	// Write updated message back
   384→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   385→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   392→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from LastCheckpointID.
   393→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   394→	// Read current commit message
   395→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	message := string(content)
   401→
   402→	// If message already has a trailer, keep it unchanged
   403→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   404→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend preserves existing trailer",
   405→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   406→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   407→		)
   408→		return nil
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	// No trailer in message — check if any session has LastCheckpointID to restore
   412→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   413→	if err != nil {
```
