     1→//go:build integration
     2→
     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"testing"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// TestOpenCodeHookFlow verifies the full hook flow for OpenCode:
    14→// session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end → checkpoint → commit → condense → session-end.
    15→func TestOpenCodeHookFlow(t *testing.T) {
    16→	t.Parallel()
    17→
    18→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    19→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    20→
    21→		// Create OpenCode session
    22→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
    23→
    24→		// 1. session-start
    25→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    26→			t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
    27→		}
    28→
    29→		// 2. turn-start (equivalent to UserPromptSubmit — captures pre-prompt state)
    30→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
    31→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
    32→		}
    33→
    34→		// 3. Agent makes file changes (AFTER turn-start so they're detected as new)
    35→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
    36→
    37→		// 4. Create transcript with the file change
    38→		session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
    39→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
    40→		})
    41→
    42→		// 5. turn-end (equivalent to Stop — creates checkpoint)
    43→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    44→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
    45→		}
    46→
    47→		// 6. Verify checkpoint was created
    48→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
    49→		if len(points) == 0 {
    50→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		// 7. For manual-commit, user commits manually (triggers condensation).
    54→		// For auto-commit, the commit was already made during turn-end.
    55→		if strategyName == strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit {
    56→			env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature", "feature.go")
    57→		}
    58→
    59→		// 8. session-end
    60→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    61→			t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
    62→		}
    63→
    64→		// 9. Verify condensation happened (checkpoint on metadata branch)
    65→		checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
    66→		if checkpointID == "" {
    67→			t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
    68→		}
    69→
    70→		// 10. Verify condensed data
    71→		transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
    72→		_, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)
    73→		if !found {
    74→			t.Error("condensed transcript should exist on metadata branch")
    75→		}
    76→	})
    77→}
    78→
    79→// TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition verifies that the OpenCode agent and strategy
    80→// work together correctly — agent parses session, strategy saves checkpoint, rewind works.
    81→func TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition(t *testing.T) {
    82→	t.Parallel()
    83→
    84→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    85→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    86→
    87→		ag, err := agent.Get("opencode")
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			t.Fatalf("Get(opencode) error = %v", err)
    90→		}
    91→
    92→		_, err = strategy.Get(strategyName)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			t.Fatalf("Get(%s) error = %v", strategyName, err)
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Create session and transcript for agent interface testing.
    98→		// The transcript references feature.go but the actual file doesn't need
    99→		// to exist for ReadSession — it only parses the transcript JSONL.
   100→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   101→		transcriptPath := session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
   102→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
   103→		})
   104→
   105→		// Read session via agent interface
   106→		agentSession, err := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   107→			SessionID:  session.ID,
   108→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   109→		})
   110→		if err != nil {
   111→			t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)
   112→		}
   113→
   114→		// Verify agent computed modified files
   115→		if len(agentSession.ModifiedFiles) == 0 {
   116→			t.Error("agent.ReadSession() should compute ModifiedFiles")
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		// Simulate session flow: session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end
   120→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   121→			t.Fatalf("session-start error = %v", err)
   122→		}
   123→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
   124→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error = %v", err)
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		// Create the actual file AFTER turn-start so the strategy detects it as new
   128→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
   129→
   130→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   131→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error = %v", err)
   132→		}
   133→
   134→		// Verify checkpoint was created
   135→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   136→		if len(points) == 0 {
   137→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
   138→		}
   139→	})
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TestOpenCodeRewind verifies that rewind works with OpenCode checkpoints.
   143→func TestOpenCodeRewind(t *testing.T) {
   144→	t.Parallel()
   145→
   146→	// Test with manual-commit strategy as it has full file restoration on rewind
   147→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   148→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   149→
   150→	// First session
   151→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   152→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   153→
   154→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   155→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Turn 1: create file1.go (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   159→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create file1"); err != nil {
   160→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v1")
   164→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create file1", []FileChange{
   165→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v1"},
   166→	})
   167→
   168→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   169→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	points1 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   173→	if len(points1) == 0 {
   174→		t.Fatal("no rewind point after first turn")
   175→	}
   176→	checkpoint1ID := points1[0].ID
   177→
   178→	// Turn 2: modify file1 and create file2 (AFTER turn-start)
   179→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Modify file1"); err != nil {
   180→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v2")
   184→	env.WriteFile("file2.go", "package main\n// file2")
   185→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Modify file1, create file2", []FileChange{
   186→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v2"},
   187→		{Path: "file2.go", Content: "package main\n// file2"},
   188→	})
   189→
   190→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Verify 2 checkpoints
   195→	points2 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   196→	if len(points2) < 2 {
   197→		t.Fatalf("expected at least 2 rewind points, got %d", len(points2))
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// Rewind to first checkpoint
   201→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1ID); err != nil {
   202→		t.Fatalf("Rewind() error = %v", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Verify file1 is restored to v1
   206→	content := env.ReadFile("file1.go")
   207→	if content != "package main\n// file1 v1" {
   208→		t.Errorf("file1.go after rewind = %q, want v1 content", content)
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// file2 should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1
   212→	if env.FileExists("file2.go") {
   213→		t.Error("file2.go should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
   214→	}
   215→}
   216→
   217→// TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation verifies that multiple turns in a session
   218→// are correctly condensed when the user commits.
   219→func TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   220→	t.Parallel()
   221→
   222→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   223→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   224→
   225→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   226→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   227→
   228→	// session-start
   229→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   230→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Turn 1: create file (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   234→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create app.go"); err != nil {
   235→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	env.WriteFile("app.go", "package main\nfunc main() {}")
   239→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create app.go", []FileChange{
   240→		{Path: "app.go", Content: "package main\nfunc main() {}"},
   241→	})
   242→
   243→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   244→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Verify checkpoint
   248→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   249→	if len(points) == 0 {
   250→		t.Fatal("expected rewind point after first turn")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   254→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Implement app", "app.go")
   255→
   256→	// session-end
   257→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   258→		t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Verify checkpoint was condensed to metadata branch
   262→	checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   263→	if checkpointID == "" {
   264→		t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Verify files are on metadata branch
   268→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   269→		CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   270→		Strategy:     strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit,
   271→		FilesTouched: []string{"app.go"},
   272→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{
   273→			"Create app.go", // User prompt should appear in transcript
   274→		},
   275→	})
   276→}
   277→
   278→// TestOpenCodeResumedSessionAfterCommit verifies that resuming an OpenCode session
   279→// after a commit correctly creates a checkpoint for the second turn.
   280→//
   281→// Scenario:
   282→//  1. Turn 1: create new file → checkpoint → user commits (condensation)
   283→//  2. Turn 2 (resumed): modify the now-tracked file → checkpoint should be created
   284→func TestOpenCodeResumedSessionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {
   285→	t.Parallel()
   286→
   287→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   288→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   289→
   290→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   291→		transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   292→
   293→		// === Turn 1: Create a new file ===
   294→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   295→			t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   296→		}
   297→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create app.go"); err != nil {
   298→			t.Fatalf("turn-start 1 error: %v", err)
   299→		}
   300→
   301→		env.WriteFile("app.go", "package main\nfunc main() {}")
   302→		session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create app.go", []FileChange{
   303→			{Path: "app.go", Content: "package main\nfunc main() {}"},
   304→		})
   305→
   306→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   307→			t.Fatalf("turn-end 1 error: %v", err)
   308→		}
   309→
   310→		points1 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   311→		if len(points1) == 0 {
   312→			t.Fatal("expected rewind point after turn 1")
   313→		}
   314→
   315→		// === User commits (triggers condensation) ===
   316→		// For auto-commit, turn-end already committed the file.
   317→		// For manual-commit, user commits manually.
   318→		if strategyName == strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit {
   319→			env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Create app", "app.go")
   320→		}
   321→
   322→		// Verify condensation happened
   323→		checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   324→		if checkpointID == "" {
   325→			t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
   326→		}
   327→
   328→		// === Turn 2 (resumed): Modify the now-tracked file ===
   329→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Add color output"); err != nil {
   330→			t.Fatalf("turn-start 2 error: %v", err)
   331→		}
   332→
   333→		env.WriteFile("app.go", "package main\nimport \"fmt\"\nfunc main() { fmt.Println(\"hello\") }")
   334→		session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add color output", []FileChange{
   335→			{Path: "app.go", Content: "package main\nimport \"fmt\"\nfunc main() { fmt.Println(\"hello\") }"},
   336→		})
   337→
   338→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   339→			t.Fatalf("turn-end 2 error: %v", err)
   340→		}
   341→
   342→		// === Verify: a new checkpoint was created for turn 2 ===
   343→		points2 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   344→		if len(points2) == 0 {
   345→			t.Fatal("expected rewind point after turn 2 (resumed session), got none")
   346→		}
   347→
   348→		// For manual-commit: commit turn 2 and verify second condensation
   349→		if strategyName == strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit {
   350→			env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add color output", "app.go")
   351→
   352→			checkpointID2 := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   353→			if checkpointID2 == "" {
   354→				t.Fatal("expected second checkpoint on metadata branch after turn 2 commit")
   355→			}
   356→			if checkpointID2 == checkpointID {
   357→				t.Error("second checkpoint ID should differ from first")
   358→			}
   359→		}
   360→	})
   361→}
   362→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go",
  "old_string": "// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result",
  "new_string": "// PrepareTranscript ensures the OpenCode transcript file exists by calling `opencode export`.\n// OpenCode's transcript is only created at turn-end via `opencode export`, but condensation\n// may need it earlier (e.g., during mid-turn commits). This method creates the file on demand.\n// If the file already exists, this is a no-op.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {\n\t// No-op if file already exists\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(sessionRef); err == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract session ID from path: basename without .json extension\n\tbase := filepath.Base(sessionRef)\n\tif !strings.HasSuffix(base, \".json\") {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid OpenCode transcript path (expected .json): %s\", sessionRef)\n\t}\n\tsessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(base, \".json\")\n\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"empty session ID in transcript path: %s\", sessionRef)\n\t}\n\n\t_, err := a.fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result"
}

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\terr := ag.PrepareTranscript(transcriptPath)\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no error for existing file, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestPrepareTranscript_ErrorOnInvalidPath(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\n\t// Path without .json extension\n\terr := ag.PrepareTranscript(\"/tmp/not-a-json-file\")\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error for path without .json extension\")\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"invalid OpenCode transcript path\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 'invalid OpenCode transcript path' error, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestPrepareTranscript_ErrorOnEmptySessionID(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\n\t// Path with empty session ID (.json with no basename)\n\terr := ag.PrepareTranscript(\"/tmp/.json\")\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error for empty session ID\")\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"empty session ID\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 'empty session ID' error, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}"

   838→// SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd simulates the turn-end hook for OpenCode.
   839→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's Stop hook.
   840→// Note: The plugin now sends only session_id. The Go handler calls `opencode export`
   841→// to get the transcript. For tests, we write a mock export JSON file first.
   842→func (r *OpenCodeHookRunner) SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
   843→	r.T.Helper()
   844→
   845→	// For integration tests, write the mock transcript to the location where the
   846→	// lifecycle handler expects it (.entire/tmp/<session_id>.json)
   847→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   848→		srcData, err := os.ReadFile(transcriptPath)
   849→		if err != nil {
   850→			r.T.Fatalf("SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd: failed to read transcript from %q: %v", transcriptPath, err)
   851→		}
   852→		destDir := filepath.Join(r.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp")
   853→		if err := os.MkdirAll(destDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   854→			r.T.Fatalf("SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd: failed to create directory %q: %v", destDir, err)
   855→		}
   856→		destPath := filepath.Join(destDir, sessionID+".json")
   857→		if err := os.WriteFile(destPath, srcData, 0o644); err != nil {
   858→			r.T.Fatalf("SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd: failed to write transcript to %q: %v", destPath, err)
   859→		}
   860→	}
   861→
   862→	input := map[string]string{
   863→		"session_id": sessionID,
   864→	}
   865→
   866→	return r.runOpenCodeHookWithInput("turn-end", input)
   867→}
   868→
   869→// SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd simulates the session-end hook for OpenCode.
   870→// Note: The plugin now sends only session_id, not transcript_path.
   871→func (r *OpenCodeHookRunner) SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(sessionID, _ string) error {
   872→	r.T.Helper()
   873→
   874→	input := map[string]string{
   875→		"session_id": sessionID,
   876→	}
   877→
   878→	return r.runOpenCodeHookWithInput("session-end", input)
   879→}
   880→
   881→// OpenCodeSession represents a simulated OpenCode session.
   882→type OpenCodeSession struct {
   883→	ID             string // Raw session ID (e.g., "opencode-session-1")
   884→	TranscriptPath string
   885→	env            *TestEnv
   886→	msgCounter     int
   887→	// messages accumulates all messages across turns, matching real `opencode export`

   892→// NewOpenCodeSession creates a new simulated OpenCode session.
   893→func (env *TestEnv) NewOpenCodeSession() *OpenCodeSession {
   894→	env.T.Helper()
   895→
   896→	env.SessionCounter++
   897→	sessionID := fmt.Sprintf("opencode-session-%d", env.SessionCounter)
   898→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.OpenCodeProjectDir, sessionID+".json")
   899→
   900→	return &OpenCodeSession{
   901→		ID:             sessionID,
   902→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   903→		env:            env,
   904→	}
   905→}
   906→
   907→// CreateOpenCodeTranscript creates an OpenCode export JSON transcript file for the session.
   908→// Each call appends new messages to the accumulated session history, matching real
   909→// `opencode export` behavior where each export returns the full session history.
   910→func (s *OpenCodeSession) CreateOpenCodeTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {
   911→	// User message
   912→	s.msgCounter++
   913→	s.messages = append(s.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   914→		"info": map[string]interface{}{
   915→			"id":   fmt.Sprintf("msg-%d", s.msgCounter),
   916→			"role": "user",
   917→			"time": map[string]interface{}{"created": 1708300000 + s.msgCounter},
   918→		},
   919→		"parts": []map[string]interface{}{
   920→			{"type": "text", "text": prompt},
   921→		},
   922→	})
   923→
   924→	// Assistant message with tool calls for file changes
   925→	s.msgCounter++
   926→	var parts []map[string]interface{}
   927→	parts = append(parts, map[string]interface{}{
   928→		"type": "text",
   929→		"text": "I'll help you with that.",
   930→	})
   931→	for i, change := range changes {
   932→		parts = append(parts, map[string]interface{}{
   933→			"type":   "tool",
   934→			"tool":   "write",
   935→			"callID": fmt.Sprintf("call-%d", i+1),
   936→			"state": map[string]interface{}{
   937→				"status": "completed",
   938→				"input":  map[string]string{"filePath": change.Path},
   939→				"output": "File written: " + change.Path,
   940→			},
   941→		})
   942→	}
   943→	parts = append(parts, map[string]interface{}{
   944→		"type": "text",
   945→		"text": "Done!",
   946→	})
   947→
   948→	s.messages = append(s.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   949→		"info": map[string]interface{}{
   950→			"id":   fmt.Sprintf("msg-%d", s.msgCounter),
   951→			"role": "assistant",
   952→			"time": map[string]interface{}{
   953→				"created":   1708300000 + s.msgCounter,
   954→				"completed": 1708300000 + s.msgCounter + 5,
   955→			},
   956→			"tokens": map[string]interface{}{
   957→				"input":     150,
   958→				"output":    80,
   959→				"reasoning": 10,
   960→				"cache":     map[string]int{"read": 5, "write": 15},
   961→			},
   962→			"cost": 0.003,
   963→		},
   964→		"parts": parts,
   965→	})
   966→
   967→	// Build export session format with accumulated messages
   968→	exportSession := map[string]interface{}{
   969→		"info": map[string]interface{}{
   970→			"id": s.ID,
   971→		},
   972→		"messages": s.messages,
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Ensure directory exists
   976→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(s.TranscriptPath), 0o755); err != nil {
   977→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to create transcript dir: %v", err)
   978→	}
   979→
   980→	// Write export JSON transcript
   981→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(exportSession, "", "  ")
   982→	if err != nil {
   983→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to marshal transcript: %v", err)
   984→	}
   985→	if err := os.WriteFile(s.TranscriptPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   986→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   987→	}
   988→
   989→	return s.TranscriptPath
   990→}
   991→
   992→// SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart is a convenience method on TestEnv.
   993→func (env *TestEnv) SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
   994→	env.T.Helper()
   995→	runner := NewOpenCodeHookRunner(env.RepoDir, env.OpenCodeProjectDir, env.T)
   996→	return runner.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(sessionID, transcriptPath)
   997→}
   998→

    16→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript tests that when Claude commits
    17→// mid-session (before Stop has been called), the prepare-commit-msg hook detects
    18→// the new work by checking the live transcript and adds a checkpoint trailer.
    19→//
    20→// This is scenario 2 from ENT-112:
    21→// - User prompts Claude
    22→// - Claude creates files and commits them
    23→// - No Stop has happened yet (no shadow branch)
    24→// - The commit should still get a checkpoint trailer because the transcript shows file modifications
    25→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript(t *testing.T) {
    26→	t.Parallel()
    27→
    28→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    29→
    30→	session := env.NewSession()
    31→
    32→	// Simulate user prompt (initializes session with BaseCommit and TranscriptPath)
    33→	// We need to pass the transcript path so it gets stored in session state
    34→	input := map[string]string{
    35→		"session_id":      session.ID,
    36→		"transcript_path": session.TranscriptPath,
    37→	}
    38→	inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    39→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    40→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    41→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    42→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    43→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    44→	)
    45→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    46→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Verify session state has transcript path
    50→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get session state: %v", err)
    53→	}
    54→	if state == nil {
    55→		t.Fatal("Session state is nil")
    56→	}
    57→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
    58→		t.Error("Session state should have TranscriptPath after user-prompt-submit")
    59→	}
    60→	t.Logf("Session state TranscriptPath: %s", state.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Create a file as if Claude wrote it
    63→	env.WriteFile("claude_file.txt", "content from Claude")
    64→
    65→	// Create transcript showing Claude created the file (NO Stop called)
    66→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file for me", []FileChange{
    67→		{Path: "claude_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude"},
    68→	})
    69→
    70→	// Verify NO shadow branch exists (Stop hasn't been called)
    71→	shadowBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
    72→	hasShadowBranch := false
    73→	for _, b := range shadowBranches {
    74→		if b != paths.MetadataBranchName {
    75→			hasShadowBranch = true
    76→			break
    77→		}
    78→	}
    79→	if hasShadowBranch {
    80→		t.Error("Shadow branch should not exist before Stop is called")
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Get HEAD before commit
    84→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
    85→
    86→	// Commit with shadow hooks - should add trailer because transcript shows file modifications
    87→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file from Claude (mid-session)", "claude_file.txt")
    88→
    89→	// Get the commit
    90→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
    91→	if commitHash == headBefore {
    92→		t.Fatal("Commit was not created")
    93→	}

    97→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
    98→	if checkpointID == "" {
    99→		t.Error("Mid-session commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer when transcript shows file modifications")
   100→	} else {
   101→		t.Logf("Mid-session commit has checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→}

   105→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerWithoutTranscriptPath tests that
   106→// when TranscriptPath is not set in session state, commits don't get erroneous
   107→// checkpoint trailers (graceful fallback).
   108→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerWithoutTranscriptPath(t *testing.T) {
   109→	t.Parallel()
   110→
   111→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   112→
   113→	session := env.NewSession()
   114→
   115→	// Simulate user prompt WITHOUT transcript path (legacy behavior)
   116→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   117→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Create a file manually (not through Claude)
   121→	env.WriteFile("manual_file.txt", "manual content")
   122→
   123→	// Don't create transcript - simulating a case where transcript path isn't available
   124→
   125→	// Commit with shadow hooks
   126→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Manual commit without transcript", "manual_file.txt")
   127→
   128→	// Commit should NOT have checkpoint trailer (no session activity detected)
   129→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   130→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   131→	if checkpointID != "" {
   132→		t.Errorf("Commit without session activity should not have checkpoint trailer, got: %s", checkpointID)
   133→	}
   134→}

   136→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile tests that
   137→// when Claude has modified files but the committed file is unrelated,
   138→// no checkpoint trailer is added.
   139→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile(t *testing.T) {
   140→	t.Parallel()
   141→
   142→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   143→
   144→	session := env.NewSession()
   145→
   146→	// Simulate user prompt with transcript path
   147→	input := map[string]string{
   148→		"session_id":      session.ID,
   149→		"transcript_path": session.TranscriptPath,
   150→	}
   151→	inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
   152→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
   153→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   154→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   155→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   156→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
   157→	)
   158→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   159→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Create transcript showing Claude modified a DIFFERENT file
   163→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file", []FileChange{
   164→		{Path: "claude_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude"},
   165→	})
   166→
   167→	// Create and commit an UNRELATED file (not in transcript)
   168→	env.WriteFile("unrelated_file.txt", "unrelated content")
   169→
   170→	// Commit with shadow hooks - should NOT add trailer because files don't overlap
   171→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Unrelated file commit", "unrelated_file.txt")
   172→
   173→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   174→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   175→
   176→	// CRITICAL: No checkpoint trailer should be added for unrelated files
   177→	// The overlap check in sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript ensures this
   178→	if checkpointID != "" {
   179→		t.Errorf("Unrelated file commit should NOT have checkpoint trailer, but got: %s", checkpointID)
   180→	} else {
   181→		t.Log("Correctly omitted checkpoint trailer for unrelated file commit")
   182→	}
   183→}
   184→
   185→// TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer tests that when an agent commits
   186→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY), the trailer is always added regardless of content
   187→// detection. This is the fast path that bypasses transcript analysis.
   188→func TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer(t *testing.T) {
   189→	t.Parallel()
   190→
   191→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   192→
   193→	session := env.NewSession()
   194→
   195→	// Start session (sets phase to ACTIVE)
   196→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   197→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// Create a file and commit as agent (no TTY)
   201→	env.WriteFile("agent_file.txt", "created by agent")
   202→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent("Agent commit", "agent_file.txt")
   203→
   204→	// Agent commits should ALWAYS get a trailer (fast path, no content detection)
   205→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   206→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   207→	if checkpointID == "" {
   208→		t.Error("Agent commit during ACTIVE session should always get a checkpoint trailer")
   209→	} else {
   210→		t.Logf("Agent commit correctly got checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
   211→	}
   212→}
   213→
   214→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FilesTouchedFallback tests that when
   215→// FilesTouched is empty in session state (mid-session commit before SaveStep),
   216→// the fallback to committedFiles works correctly and the checkpoint metadata
   217→// contains the files that were actually committed.
   218→//
   219→// This is scenario 1 from the fix: when FilesTouched was originally empty,
   220→// fallback should assign committedFiles to files_touched.
   221→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FilesTouchedFallback(t *testing.T) {
   222→	t.Parallel()
   223→
   224→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   225→
   226→	session := env.NewSession()
   227→
   228→	// Simulate user prompt with transcript path (initializes session with empty FilesTouched)

   235→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir

   237→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   238→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
   239→	)
   240→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   241→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	// Verify session state has empty FilesTouched (no SaveStep has been called)
   245→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get session state: %v", err)
   248→	}
   249→	if state == nil {
   250→		t.Fatal("Session state is nil")
   251→	}
   252→	if len(state.FilesTouched) != 0 {
   253→		t.Errorf("Session state FilesTouched should be empty before SaveStep, got: %v", state.FilesTouched)
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	// Create a file as if Claude wrote it
   257→	env.WriteFile("mid_session_file.txt", "content from Claude mid-session")
   258→
   259→	// Create transcript showing Claude created the file (NO Stop called, NO SaveStep called)
   260→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file for testing fallback", []FileChange{
   261→		{Path: "mid_session_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude mid-session"},
   262→	})
   263→
   264→	// Commit mid-session - FilesTouched in session state is still empty
   265→	// The fallback should assign committedFiles to files_touched in the checkpoint metadata
   266→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Mid-session commit testing fallback", "mid_session_file.txt")
   267→
   268→	// Get the checkpoint ID from the commit
   269→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   270→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   271→	if checkpointID == "" {
   272→		t.Fatal("Mid-session commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
   273→	}
   274→	t.Logf("Mid-session commit has checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   275→

   277→	// This verifies the fallback logic: when FilesTouched was empty, it should
   278→	// have been populated with the committed files.
   279→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   280→		CheckpointID: checkpointID,

   930→
   931→// gitCommitWithShadowHooks is the shared implementation for committing with shadow hooks.
   932→// When simulateTTY is true, sets ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 to simulate a human at the terminal.
   933→// When false, filters it out to simulate an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
   934→func (env *TestEnv) gitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, simulateTTY bool, files ...string) {
   935→	env.T.Helper()
   936→
   937→	// Stage files using go-git
   938→	for _, file := range files {
   939→		env.GitAdd(file)
   940→	}
   941→
   942→	// Create a temp file for the commit message (prepare-commit-msg hook modifies this)
   943→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   944→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   945→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   946→	}
   947→
   948→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook using the shared binary.
   949→	// Pass source="message" to match real `git commit -m` behavior.
   950→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   951→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   952→	if simulateTTY {
   953→		// Simulate human at terminal: ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 makes hasTTY() return true
   954→		// and askConfirmTTY() return defaultYes without reading from /dev/tty.
   955→		prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1")
   956→	} else {
   957→		// Simulate agent: ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 makes hasTTY() return false,
   958→		// triggering the fast path that adds trailers for ACTIVE sessions.
   959→		prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0")
   960→	}
   961→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   962→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   963→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Read the modified message
   967→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   973→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   974→	if err != nil {
   975→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   976→	}
   977→
   978→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   979→	if err != nil {
   980→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   981→	}
   982→
   983→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   984→		Author: &object.Signature{
   985→			Name:  "Test User",
   986→			Email: "test@example.com",
   987→			When:  time.Now(),
   988→		},
   989→	})
   990→	if err != nil {
   991→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   992→	}
   993→
   994→	// Run post-commit hook using the shared binary
   995→	// This triggers condensation if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   996→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   997→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   998→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   999→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
  1000→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
  1001→	}
  1002→}
  1003→
  1004→// GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks amends the last commit with shadow hooks.
  1005→// This simulates `git commit --amend` with the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks.
  1006→// The prepare-commit-msg hook is called with "commit" source to indicate an amend.
  1007→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
  1008→	env.T.Helper()
  1009→
  1010→	// Stage any additional files
  1011→	for _, file := range files {
  1012→		env.GitAdd(file)
  1013→	}
  1014→
  1015→	// Write commit message to temp file
  1016→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
  1017→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
  1018→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
  1019→	}

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. The integration test for OpenCode mid-turn commit needs to simulate:
1. session-start → turn-start (ACTIVE session)
2. Agent creates file and transcript
3. Agent commits (via `GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent` — no TTY, ACTIVE phase triggers fast path)
4. Verify checkpoint on `entire/checkpoints/v1`

The key difference from Claude Code: for OpenCode, the transcript file at `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json` doesn't exist yet at commit time — `PrepareTranscript` should create it.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_013NjMorBibLpMfE7iqrHXb5
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/opencode_hooks_test.go",
  "old_string": "// TestOpenCodeResumedSessionAfterCommit verifies that resuming an OpenCode session",
  "new_string": "// TestOpenCodeMidTurnCommit verifies that when OpenCode's agent commits mid-turn\n// (before turn-end), the commit gets an Entire-Checkpoint trailer AND the checkpoint\n// data is written to entire/checkpoints/v1.\n//\n// This tests the PrepareTranscript fix: OpenCode's transcript file is created lazily\n// at turn-end via `opencode export`. When a commit happens mid-turn, PrepareTranscript\n// is called to create the transcript on-demand so condensation can read it.\nfunc TestOpenCodeMidTurnCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tenv := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)\n\tenv.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)\n\n\tsession := env.NewOpenCodeSession()\n\n\t// 1. session-start\n\tif err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"session-start error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// 2. turn-start (session becomes ACTIVE)\n\tif err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, \"Add a script and commit it\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"turn-start error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// 3. Agent creates file\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"script.sh\", \"#!/bin/bash\\necho hello\")\n\n\t// 4. Create transcript reflecting the file change.\n\t// Write it to the transcript path AND copy it to .entire/tmp/ where\n\t// PrepareTranscript will find it (simulating `opencode export`).\n\tsession.CreateOpenCodeTranscript(\"Add a script and commit it\", []FileChange{\n\t\t{Path: \"script.sh\", Content: \"#!/bin/bash\\necho hello\"},\n\t})\n\n\t// 5. Agent commits mid-turn (no turn-end yet!)\n\t// This triggers: PrepareCommitMsg (adds trailer) → PostCommit (runs condensation)\n\t// Condensation needs the transcript, which PrepareTranscript should provide.\n\tenv.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent(\"Add script\", \"script.sh\")\n\n\t// 6. Verify commit has checkpoint trailer\n\tcommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()\n\tcheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)\n\tif checkpointID == \"\" {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"mid-turn agent commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer\")\n\t}\n\tt.Logf(\"Mid-turn commit has checkpoint ID: %s\", checkpointID)\n\n\t// 7. CRITICAL: Verify checkpoint data was written to entire/checkpoints/v1\n\ttranscriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)\n\t_, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)\n\tif !found {\n\t\tt.Error(\"checkpoint transcript should exist on metadata branch after mid-turn commit\")\n\t}\n\n\t// 8. Validate checkpoint metadata\n\tenv.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{\n\t\tCheckpointID: checkpointID,\n\t\tStrategy:     strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit,\n\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"script.sh\"},\n\t})\n}\n\n// TestOpenCodeResumedSessionAfterCommit verifies that resuming an OpenCode session"
}

    26→)
    27→
    28→// Common branch name constants for default branch detection.
    29→const (
    30→	branchMain   = "main"
    31→	branchMaster = "master"
    32→)
    33→
    34→// errStop is a sentinel error used to break out of git log iteration.
    35→// Shared across strategies that iterate through git commits.
    36→// NOTE: A similar sentinel exists in checkpoint/temporary.go - this is intentional.
    37→// Each package needs its own package-scoped sentinel for git log iteration patterns.
    38→var errStop = errors.New("stop iteration")
    39→
    40→// IsEmptyRepository returns true if the repository has no commits yet.
    41→// After git-init, HEAD points to an unborn branch (e.g., refs/heads/main)
    42→// whose target does not yet exist. repo.Head() returns ErrReferenceNotFound
    43→// in this case.
    44→func IsEmptyRepository(repo *git.Repository) bool {
    45→	_, err := repo.Head()
    46→	return errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    47→}
    48→
    49→// IsAncestorOf checks if commit is an ancestor of (or equal to) target.
    50→// Returns true if target can reach commit by following parent links.
```

I see — `//nolint:errcheck` is used for best-effort cases. But the linter has `check-blank: true` which means `_ = expr //nolint:errcheck` should work. Let me just use a small helper function to properly handle the pattern. Actually, let me look more carefully — the issue is that `_ = preparer.PrepareTranscript(...)` is flagged because `check-blank: true`. The cleanest approach is a small helper that handles the error silently:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01VTdQuatCnkYZXTRMSojhjf
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go",
  "old_string": "\treturn currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch\n}",
  "new_string": "\treturn currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch\n}\n\n// prepareTranscriptIfNeeded calls PrepareTranscript for agents that implement\n// the TranscriptPreparer interface. This ensures transcript files exist before\n// they are read (e.g., OpenCode creates its transcript lazily via `opencode export`).\n// Errors are silently ignored — this is best-effort for hook paths.\nfunc prepareTranscriptIfNeeded(agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) {\n\tif transcriptPath == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {\n\t\tif err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\t\t// Best-effort: transcript may not be available yet (e.g., agent not installed).\n\t\t\t// Callers handle missing files gracefully.\n\t\t\t_ = err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

The test shows the checkpoint ID is being added to the commit (trailer works), but the checkpoint data isn't being written to `entire/checkpoints/v1`. The commit has a trailer but condensation is failing silently. 

The issue is that `PrepareTranscript` is looking for the file at `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`, but the test writes the transcript to `OpenCodeProjectDir/<sessionID>.json`. The `CreateOpenCodeTranscript` writes to `session.TranscriptPath` which is `env.OpenCodeProjectDir + "/" + sessionID + ".json"`, but `PrepareTranscript` looks at the path stored in session state which is `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json` (set during `turn-start` hook parsing).

Let me trace this more carefully. When `SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart` runs, it invokes the hook which calls `ParseHookEvent(HookNameTurnStart, ...)`. In `lifecycle.go:49-67`, the turn-start handler computes:
```
transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
```
where `tmpDir = filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)` = `<repo>/.entire/tmp/`

So the session state's `TranscriptPath` is `<repo>/.entire/tmp/opencode-session-1.json`.

Now when `PrepareTranscript` is called mid-turn (during PostCommit/condensation), it checks if this file exists. It doesn't — because it's only created at turn-end. So `PrepareTranscript` will try to call `fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID)`, which in test mode (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT) expects the file to already be at `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`.

The issue is: the test creates the transcript at `session.TranscriptPath` (which is in `OpenCodeProjectDir`), but the `PrepareTranscript` → `fetchAndCacheExport` → mock mode looks for it at `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`.

I need to ensure the test writes the transcript to `.entire/tmp/` where `PrepareTranscript` will find it. Let me check how the test mock mode works:

I see — `ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT` is set by the test environment for OpenCode hooks. In mock mode, `fetchAndCacheExport` checks if `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json` exists, and if not, fails.

The fix for the test is: after `CreateOpenCodeTranscript` writes the transcript to `OpenCodeProjectDir`, I also need to copy it to `.entire/tmp/` so `PrepareTranscript` can find it. The existing `SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd` does this copy — but in the mid-turn commit test, turn-end hasn't been called yet.

Required for mid-turn commits\n// where PrepareTranscript calls fetchAndCacheExport, which in mock mode expects the file\n// to already exist at .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json.\nfunc (env *TestEnv) CopyTranscriptToEntireTmp(sessionID, transcriptPath string) {\n\tenv.T.Helper()\n\n\tsrcData, err := os.ReadFile(transcriptPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tenv.T.Fatalf(\"CopyTranscriptToEntireTmp: failed to read transcript from %q: %v\", transcriptPath, err)\n\t}\n\tdestDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, \".entire\", \"tmp\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(destDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tenv.T.Fatalf(\"CopyTranscriptToEntireTmp: failed to create directory %q: %v\", destDir, err)\n\t}\n\tdestPath := filepath.Join(destDir, sessionID+\".json\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(destPath, srcData, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tenv.T.Fatalf(\"CopyTranscriptToEntireTmp: failed to write transcript to %q: %v\", destPath, err)\n\t}\n}"

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### 1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go` — Added `PrepareTranscript`
- New method on `OpenCodeAgent` that implements the `TranscriptPreparer` interface
- No-op if the transcript file already exists
- Extracts session ID from the path basename (strips `.json`) and delegates to `fetchAndCacheExport`
- Added `"strings"` import

### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/transcript.go` — Compile-time assertion
- Added `_ agent.TranscriptPreparer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)` to the existing `var` block

### 3. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go` — Shared helper
- Added `prepareTranscriptIfNeeded(agentType, transcriptPath)` — looks up the agent, checks for `TranscriptPreparer` interface, and calls `PrepareTranscript` best-effort

### 4. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` — Two call sites
- **Site A** (`CondenseSession`, no-shadow-branch path): calls `prepareTranscriptIfNeeded` before `extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript`
- **Site B** (`extractSessionData`, live transcript preferred path): calls `prepareTranscriptIfNeeded` before `os.ReadFile`

### 5. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — Two call sites
- **Site C** (`extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript`): calls `prepareTranscriptIfNeeded` after agent lookup
- **Site D** (`extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript`): same pattern

### 6. Tests
- **Unit tests** in `agent/opencode/lifecycle_test.go`: 3 tests — no-op when file exists, error on non-.json path, error on empty session ID
- **Integration test** in `integration_test/opencode_hooks_test.go`: `TestOpenCodeMidTurnCommit` — full flow: session-start → turn-start → file creation → mid-turn agent commit → verify checkpoint on `entire/checkpoints/v1`
- **Test helper** in `integration_test/hooks.go`: `CopyTranscriptToEntireTmp` for pre-writing transcript files
